HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_10929e1d6ee9c3a09408","text":"3. **The allocation can assign separate reckoning and recompense.** Fixed `د ي ن B002` supplies judgment, reckoning, and requital. The two **لـ** phrases assign what follows to “you” and “me,” and the preceding clauses supply differentiated conduct. I infer the causal arrow from service-practice to accountable outcome; the packet gives conduct, branch, and allocation but does not state that arrow. The line can therefore look forward from separate worship to separately borne reckoning, while coexisting with the obedience reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2eb39d75790dd4bf7baf","text":"3. **Debt/credit imagery makes the boundary non-transferable.** د ي ن B003, \"debt and credit,\" is activated by the possessive suffixes كُمْ and the first-person دِينِ. Mechanism: each party carries its own liability/credit account; worship cannot be exchanged or paid across the boundary. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives debt/credit and pronoun possession; I infer non-transferability from the account metaphor. Reading change: the ayah becomes a ledger-like settlement after the preceding denials.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_364cab7ea5cd48a39ff1","text":"No later ayah exists in this fixed ayah’s focus window, so no later-ayah surprise emerged. Rereading the full walk preserved the four mechanisms above because each remains anchored to fixed **دِين** and to the supplied parallel construction; their activators all come from 109:1–5 rather than from the out-of-window basmalah.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3e37c54c7afdf5e19859","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:6:2\",\"root_000504\",[\"B001\"]],[\"109:6:4\",\"root_000504\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Önceki ibadet eylem ve rollerini, her tarafa kendi zamiriyle bağlanan ayrı ve örgütlü itaat düzenleri olarak toplar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4e66003f255c060592c9","text":"1. **Distinct religion/obedience orders are allotted to distinct pronouns.** The fixed ayah supplies د ي ن B001, \"obedience and submission,\" twice in دِينُكُمْ and دِينِ. The activators are the repeated ع ب د B003 worship denials in 109:2-5. Mechanism: worship-relations crystallize into two dīn-relations, each attached to its own pronoun. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives repeated worship and final dīn nouns; I infer that dīn names the broader order generated by worship. Reading change: the final ayah is not a vague pluralism formula; it is the grammatical allotment of incompatible obedience systems.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_50402a58bd04db8d2e4f","text":"2. **Each order is also an entrenched custom.** Fixed `د ي ن B005` supplies habit and customary state. Before it, the surah cycles through imperfect service, participial servant-role, perfect prior practice, and exact repetition. Those supplied temporal and formal cues give habit a mechanism: recurrence across action, role, and prior record stabilizes a customary pattern. The stabilization arrow is inferred. **دِينُكُمْ / دِينِ** can consequently name each side’s established mode of life, not only a stated creed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6e7ca7d69ffe033d01f2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:6:2\",\"root_000504\",[\"B004\"]],[\"109:6:4\",\"root_000504\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Boyun eğdirme ve sahiplik çağrışımı açısından son paylaşım, taraflardan birinin ötekinin ibadet düzenini zorla kendine katmasını engelleyen bir sınır kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7679ab0cce667ef7ce59","text":"4. **The symmetry can enact delegation to each party’s own responsibility.** Fixed `د ي ن B007` includes crediting a person in judgment or leaving that person to his own religious responsibility. The balanced datives and possessive suffixes neither merge the two **دِين** nouns nor assign either one to the other party. This branch serves as a procedural closure: each side is left with its own accountable commitment. I infer that syntactic allocation performs that delegation. The final ayah thus reads as disciplined disengagement from mutual religious control, not as a compromise between the two ways.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_917628d6e7b1e10aeb78","text":"4. **No coercive subjugation remains after the refusal.** د ي ن B004, \"subjugation and possession,\" is activated by ع ب د B001/B004 in 109:2-5. Mechanism: the final allocation prevents either side from enslaving or owning the other's worship-relation inside this speech-act. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives subjugation/possession and worship-as-servitude branches; I infer that separation blocks coercive absorption. Reading change: the final ayah reads as a boundary against religious capture, not as indifference.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9f9fee78d5d3237ac1b1","text":"5. **The line also leaves each side to its own conscience.** د ي ن B007, \"crediting and leaving to conscience,\" is activated by the absence of further argument after the final allotment. Mechanism: once the repeated worship denials have done their work, the speech stops and leaves the addressees with their dīn. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives \"leaving to religious responsibility\"; I infer that the terminal position of the ayah performs that leaving. Reading change: the ending is a deliberate release from debate, not a failure to continue persuading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a41c80c4a2af15454580","text":"1. **The final nouns gather acts into bounded orders of obedience.** Both fixed occurrences of **دِين** activate `د ي ن B001`, obedience, submission, worship, and the way or law followed. The five preceding ayat repeatedly supply **عبد** (`ع ب د B003`), while the parallel datives and possessives, **لَكُمْ دِينُكُمْ / وَلِيَ دِينِ**, assign one order to each side. I infer that repeated acts of service consolidate into an organized way. The line therefore summarizes the preceding object contrasts as distinct allegiance-orders rather than merely private
